# Decisions Inbox — strategy-homogenization sprint

> **How this works:** Dick (or Bob) reviews cards and ratification items in a Claude Code session; the session appends dated entries below, commits with a `decisions:` prefix, and pushes. The director session pulls this file at bootstrap and acts on it: APPROVE → the fix starts from its already-failing test; DISPUTE → surfaced to Bob; RATIFY → canonical MD update lands as a PR. Nothing here executes by itself — this is the durable record of who decided what, when.

**Entry format (append below the line, newest at the bottom):**

```
## <YYYY-MM-DD> — <name>
- CARD-<n>: APPROVE | DISPUTE | NEED-MORE-INFO — <one-line note, e.g. "option 1, own-mode close">
- RATIFY <strategy_id>/<item>: RATIFIED <value or "as implemented"> | WAIVED — <note>
- ENVELOPE <strategy_id> Q<1-10>: <answer>   (Rule 21 operational-envelope answers)
```

---

## 2026-07-09 — streak-count verification addendum (director, from workflow wf_db34cac1: 3 Sonnet trace lanes + adversarial reconciler, 114 probes)
- CONFIRMED (HIGH confidence): "2" = trigger candle + 1 prior candle, single HH/HL (long) / LH/LL (short) comparison; entry price = trigger candle's own Close, never a 3rd candle's open; live == backtest BY CONSTRUCTION (single shared implementation: shadow.py:16 imports evaluate_arm/long_streak from scripts/escalator_candidate_a_backtest.py). Canonical corroboration: Escalator_Reclaimed_Shadow.md:73-74, ESCALATOR_RECLAIMED_SHADOW_LOCKDOWN.md:34, Escalator_Alpha_Rebuild_Plan.md:177-184 ("entry price rule = trigger candle close"), Escalator_Universe_Spec.md:288 ("Streak 2 / Volume SMA 10 / Trail 1.0%" — also corroborates Dick's family-wide 1% trail directive)
- Dick's "no entries before the third candle" reading: PARTIALLY CORRECT — the guard literally exists (evaluate_arm:254 loc_idx>=2, plus shadow.py:218 len(day_df)<3 refuses to run) and is one bar STRICTER than the 2-bar streak mathematically needs (unexplained margin, no code comment); moot in production because EVAL_START=10:00 binds first (4th bar of the day). Well-founded challenge, no behavioral drift.
- **NEW FINDING for Bob (from Dick's challenge; needs a ruling / possibly a new fix card):** `entry_time` is stamped at the trigger candle's OPEN while `entry_price` is that candle's CLOSE — a price not knowable until 10 minutes after the recorded entry timestamp (reconciler empirically verified pandas label=open + shadow.py:199-200 readiness gate). Downstream position sequencing correctly uses signal_ready (close), BUT `_eligible_by_cutoff` (shadow.py:383) filters the 10:40 long-entry cutoff on the OPEN-stamped entry_minute — so the last eligible trigger candle OPENS 10:40 and its signal only exists at 10:50. Canonical MD says "Entry cutoff: 10:40 ET" without defining open-vs-ready semantics. Bob to rule: is the backtested cutoff meaning "trigger candle opens by 10:40" (current behavior, backtest==live since shared code) — then document it precisely; and should entry_time labeling be made honest (T18/T19 reporting-accuracy lane)?
- Watchdog purpose CONFIRMED from scheduler_config's own description: "Runs Long Shadow through the morning entry windows so short-lived 10-minute candidates are not missed by the broader sweep" — identical generate_live_signals call as the 10-min sweep, no separate streak logic. Removal (CARD-23) does not change what is computed for a day (candidates derive from bar data, not wall-clock); whether detection latency alters live paper outcomes remains UNVERIFIED — include in Bob's watchdog review. (Caveat: the git-history provenance lane returned garbage and was discarded by the reconciler, which re-verified the substance directly; commit-level provenance of the watchdog's introduction remains unchecked.)

## 2026-07-21 — Dick O'Leary (round 28: sector-cap challenge — mechanics answered, RULEBOOK GAP found)

(b) **The same complete P&L must carry across VENUE SWITCHES: TriSight Sim → TradeStation Sim → TradeStation Live.** Bob must design the solution so P&L is wired, computed and displayed identically in all three venues, including what happens to open positions and historical rows at the moment a strategy's venue designation changes (his standing rules 1 and 3: venue is a manual terminal designation; sizing in TriSight Sim comes from backtest docs, in TradeStation venues from the gate's manual settings). Continuity of P&L across a venue change is part of the deliverable, not an afterthought.
FOR BOB: this is an architecture task, not a toggle. Bring the DESIGN to Dick before building, naming: the single P&L computation path used by all three venues; what data must start being recorded to eliminate every blank in TriSight Sim; and the venue-transition behavior for open positions and prior history.

**RULING 2 — Earnings Trader Locked 93 sizing, verbatim: "Assume $100,000 per position for TriSight sim."** => TriSight Sim paper size = **$100,000 PER POSITION**. ## 2026-07-26 — ROUND 53 DECISION-1 VALIDATION (session multi-agent review, adversarially verified HIGH confidence): stop is BRICK-anchored, NOT the pole — but brick DEPTH is pole-scaled, and the doc prose contradicts its own formula

Owner required the stop parameters validated against the sealed docs + code before answering (1a), and flagged he expected BRICK CONSOLIDATION not POLE (1b). Validated the swing family (Automated Swing / Manual Swing / High 5 — high_5_strategy.py line 18 imports automated_swing_trading and calls its detect_shark_fin; manual byte-identical). FINDING:

**IT IS THE BRICK, NOT THE POLE (verified, no code defect):** automated_swing_trading.py:1167 `consol = df.iloc[pole_idx + 1:i]` (slice starts ONE bar after the pole candle, ends before the breakout bar → structurally excludes the pole's own low, all pre-pole bars, and the breakout bar), capped to 1-7 bars (CONSOL_MIN_BARS=1 / CONSOL_MAX_BARS=7, :398-399); :1199 `consol_low = consol['Low'].min()`; :1200 `stop_price = consol_low * (1 - INITIAL_STOP_BUFFER)` = consol_low × 0.985 (:403). Adversarial verify tried to refute "brick" and could NOT (HIGH confidence). Docs match code (Automated Summary L22/L188; Manual Summary L163 defines Consolidation as "the 1 to 7 trading days immediately following the Pole candle").

**WHY THE STOP STILL LANDS ~13.83% BELOW ENTRY (the crux — his instinct was right in EFFECT):** the brick's PERMITTED DEPTH is pole-anchored. retention_floor = pole_high − (pole_body × 0.60) (:1172, POLE_GAIN_RETENTION=0.40) → the brick low may retrace up to 60% of the pole BODY below the pole high. Entry is a breakout ≥3% ABOVE the pole high (Cond 5 :1181 Close>pole_high; ENTRY_TO_POLE_HIGH_MIN=0.030 :1194-1195/:431) with NO cap on pole size (A_POLE_RANGE_MIN=0.030 is a floor only, :430). Stack: (entry above pole_high ≥3%, uncapped) + (pole_high→brick_low up to 0.60×pole_body) + (1.5% buffer). A tight brick atop a ~16%-body pole → stop/entry ≈ 0.8617 ≈ 13.83% below entry. So the stop scales with POLE HEIGHT even though it is anchored to the post-pole brick — which is why it "feels like the pole."

**DOC CONTRADICTION (a real doc defect):** Manual_Swing_Trading_Strategy_Summary.md L174 claims risk is "approximately 1% below the physical base of the setup" (tight, near-entry — matching the owner's expectation), but the doc's OWN retention-floor (60%) + ≥3% breakout-extension entry make that ~1% false for tall poles. Prose promises ~1%; the sealed parameters permit ~14%.

**DECISION 1 — reframed with evidence, still his:**
- (a) ACCEPT as sealed: the mechanics ARE the validated strategy (same math the R33 59-exit verification ran); wide-pole setups carry wide stops by the sealed formula. Consequence: FIX the misleading "~1% below the physical base" prose (L174) so the doc stops promising a tight stop the parameters don't deliver.
- (b) TIGHTEN (amendment → requires re-validation before reliance): raise POLE_GAIN_RETENTION so the brick must hold nearer the pole high (shallower brick), and/or cap pole size, and/or cap stop-distance-from-entry. Any of these changes the sealed strategy and must be re-validated (walk-forward) before he relies on it.

NET: no code bug, no wrong pattern (brick, not pole — verified). But the brick's depth is pole-scaled and the doc oversold the risk as ~1%. His call: accept the sealed wide-stop mechanics (and fix the doc prose), or tighten a parameter (amendment + re-validation). Nothing changed in code.

- So no engine (body or range) sets the stop where the owner expects; his formula is the validity floor.

Describe the two real levels plainly: (a) qualifier line = pole high (wick included) − 60% of the body; (b) the placed **stop = the consolidation's lowest low − 1.5%** (`consol_low × 0.985`), which sits on or above the qualifier line.
   - Apply the same corrections to Automated Swing / High 5 docs (shared engine).
3. **No behavior change, no re-validation.** The stops (incl. wide stops on tall poles) are the validated strategy behaving as backtested — resolves D22/D21: owner accepts them because they are validated; the fix was always the docs, not the code.
4. **Also reconcile the divergent range engines** (`bigdog_swing_production*` use High−Low; self-labeled "production-aligned" but aren't) so no backtest tool silently uses the range basis — align them to the validated body basis or clearly mark them non-authoritative.

**D8 (vocabulary):** row labels now read "Initial stop"/"Trailing stop" (#727) — the stop SIZE stays as validated per this ruling.

**NET:** Decision 1 = (ii) body/validated wins; docs amended to body; #729 rejected; no code change; no re-validation; sealed 1,636% record stands. D22/D21 resolved (stops are the validated strategy). Provenance byte-adjacent proven this round.

2. **ENTRY-WINDOW MISMATCH — CONFIRMED FIRSTHAND (his point 4).** Watchdog fires
   `hhmm_in_window(now, 1545, 15)` (`autopilot_daemon.py:795`), and `scheduler_config.py:265` computes
   `target <= current < target + window` → **15:45–15:59**. The buy gate is
   `SWING_ENTRY_START_HHMM_ET = 1545` / `SWING_ENTRY_END_HHMM_ET = 1555` exclusive
   (`swing_entry_contract.py:15,22`) and `AUTO_SWING_ENTRY_END_HHMM_ET = 1555`
   (`Target_Strategies/automated_swing_trading.py:114`) → **buys allowed 15:45–15:54 only**.
   **A 15-minute guaranteed-run window against a 10-minute buy gate: a 5-minute dead zone in which the
   watchdog fires and `auto_execute_buy:602` refuses.** Same watchdog also carries Top 40 2.0 and TS500 2.0
   into their own locked 15:45–15:55 windows — they inherit the identical exposure. Normal running fires
   within 30s of 15:45; the dead zone bites on a restart or stall crossing 15:55 — the same failure class the
   code itself documents at `autopilot_daemon.py:210-214` (intraday_pivot_sniper, 4 shorts left open 2026-04-29).

## 2026-08-07 — ROUND 74 FINAL: OWNER DECLARES THE GOVERNING DOCTRINE — VALIDATE FIRST, EVERYWHERE; all six items re-ruled under it; systemic recommendation ordered to Bob

**THE DOCTRINE (assembled from the owner's six rulings below, binding estate-wide): VALIDATION PRECEDES EVERYTHING.** No document edit, no seal, no label, no parameter adjustment, no "correction" enters the estate unless it flows from a validated run of the full process. Documents reflect the VALIDATED strategy — they are never patched ad hoc. A seal taken on an unvalidated strategy is compromised, whatever it technically covered.

**1. HIGH 5 (owner verbatim):** *"i only 'sealed parameter' because i was TOLD the strategy was validated and aligned with the md and back test documents. This current revelation indicates we need to actually validate the strategy before considering anything else. Your comments about what was sealed, checked or verified are all WRONG if the strategy has not been validated yet."* — **RULED: the High 5 validation run is ORDERED as the prerequisite to everything else on this strategy.** The 2026-07-17 parameter seal is recorded as OBTAINED ON A FALSE PREMISE (the owner was told the strategy was validated; it was not) — the seal's standing is compromised until the strategy is actually validated and the seal revisited against real numbers. The director's "parameters sealed ✓" framing is retracted.

**2. TOP 40 2.0 TOP 10 (owner verbatim):** *"you are running off the wrong cache no matter what ... how can you possibly consider a cache dated in may is even current, let alone appropriate, 3 MONTHS LATER ... this is common sense, not rocket science. Please indicate how you intend to fix this."* — **RULED: the identity guard alone is insufficient; even the sealed May-10 cache would be 3 months stale.** THE FIX (presented, Bob executes): (a) IMMEDIATELY: Top 10's outputs wear an honest UNVALIDATED-SOURCE state on their face (display truth — not a strategy change) until fixed; (b) REBUILD the score cache through the validated builder on a CURRENT window, benchmark-gated, artifacts persisted; (c) identity + age check at load (filename-match-only loading is abolished); (d) a DEFINED REFRESH CADENCE with staleness alarms so no cache silently ages again — cadence proposal comes with the rebuild plan; (e) only after the validated current cache is live does normal presentation return. If the owner wants publishing PAUSED meanwhile, one word does it; absent that word it publishes under the unvalidated-source banner.

**3. AUTOMATED SWING DAY-7 EXIT (owner verbatim):** *"so .. the recommended next step is to ACTUALLY ALIGN with the backtest and md dox that have been validated, is that correct?"* — **CONFIRMED: YES, exactly that.** The validated backtest grades the day-7 exit at end of session; live does it at ~09:35; the fix conforms live to the validated basis (late-session evaluation, High 5's evaluator reused), proven by replay parity before it ships.

**4. OAKWIND INVESTOR (owner verbatim):** *"you need to do more than edit a document, you need to revalidate the change you are recommending, which appears to be correct, BEFORE editing any documents - so that the documents actually reflected the VALIDATED strategy rather than some ad hoc edits."* — **RULED: run the capacity-capped variant as a FULL VALIDATED BACKTEST first** (the ADV-cap comparator exists only as one comparison row today); the documents are then rewritten to reflect the validated capped strategy — never annotated ad hoc. The notional-cap proposal rides the same validation.

**5. EARNINGS TRADER (owner verbatim):** *"The changes you are recommending appear to be correct, but UNTIL THEY ARE VALIDATED we will not know."* — **RULED: no relabel lands on assertion.** The forward test is DEFINED AND EXECUTED (thresholds applied to genuinely new events as the roster history accumulates, N stated); the contract document is rewritten from those validated results — in-sample vs forward performance shown from measurement, not from labels.

**6. TOP 40 2.0 §10 (owner verbatim):** *"you SHOULD NOT be adding ad hoc suggestions to 'adjust' the parameters of the strategy. If they need to be adjusted, they need to properly backtested and validated .. and then the actual strategy in production ties to a validated PROCESS and associated documents rather than simply inserting opinion items into existing documents."* — **RULED: NO ad hoc §10 rewrite.** The corrected exceedance range does not enter the document as an inserted opinion. If drawdown probability is to be stated at all, it comes from a VALIDATED estimation process (seed-robust, granularity-correct, reproducible, itself checked and committed) run end-to-end — or it is not stated. No parameter or document changes outside a validated process.

**OWNER'S SYSTEMIC CHARGE (verbatim):** *"I am sure you realize how frustrating it is to me to be addressing the same items over and over and over again ... simply because it appears you are taking shortcuts on almost over opportunity. Please think this over and recommend to Bob how you think you can improve this currently borderline unending cycle."*

**THE DIRECTOR'S RECOMMENDATION TO BOB (the cycle-breaker):** The root cause of the cycle is that the estate's unit of work has been the PATCH (edit, label, display fix) with validation deferred — every deferral returns as a new round. The unit of work must become the VALIDATED CHANGE. Concretely:
1. **A merge gate:** no PR that changes a strategy, its numbers, or its documents merges without an attached validated run (benchmark-gated, artifacts persisted, reproducible). The ONLY permissible doc-only change is marking something UNVALIDATED — asserting new numbers by edit is abolished.
2. **A per-strategy VALIDATION LADDER, on the status page, always current:** (i) performance validated (artifact + hash + date + process), (ii) parameters sealed AGAINST that validation, (iii) production-conformance verified, (iv) data inputs current (every cache/universe/roster shows its build date, max age, refresh cadence, identity check). Any rung missing → the strategy wears UNVALIDATED on its face, everywhere. High 5 and Top 10 are the proof cases: both would have been caught rounds earlier.
3. **Data-input lifecycle as law:** every derived input gets build-date-on-face, max-age policy, scheduled refresh through its validated builder, identity check at load, loud staleness state. (The May cache feeding an August strategy and the frozen universes are the same disease.)
4. **Sealing reform:** seals may only be taken when the ladder is green, and each seal records WHAT performance validation it rests on. INVENTORY the existing seals against this bar and bring the owner the honest list of which rest on real performance validation and which are parameter-only — before he is ever again told a strategy "was validated."
5. **Claim hygiene:** nothing is presented to the owner without its artifact citation; anything without one is labeled NOT VALIDATED. No invented defenses, no invented numbers, ever.
6. **Sequencing of the current queue under the doctrine:** High 5 validation run (ordered, first) → Top 10 cache rebuild + lifecycle → Automated Swing timing conformance (replay-parity-proven) → Oakwind capped-variant validation → Earnings forward test → Top 40 estimator process. Each returns to the owner as measured results, once.

**FOR BOB — execute (named):** items 1–6 above as the standing pipeline; the six strategy actions under the doctrine; and the seal inventory. Nothing in this list edits a document before its validation exists.
